# ROMS cppdefs.h — 문서와 코드 차이 (10-14 대조) 3

출처는 [빌드·전처리·옵션·변경 이력 대조 보고서](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론)의 최종 결론이다.

문서 판은 ROMS 위키 wikitext이다 (2026-05-03 받음; 쪽별 revid는 [manifest.json](../raw/manuals/wiki/manifest.json)). 코드 판은 원본 저장소 HEAD `57aecf58`이다 (2026-09-18; roms-4.2 이후 4.3 개발).

| build-options-history:RBH-1293 | POSTERIOR_ERROR_F 설명 | `Options.wiki:2465` <code>:Option to compute the final assimilation time-window posterior error covariance matrix for the &#91;&#91;Options#RBL4DVAR\|RBL4DVAR&#93;&#93; and &#91;&#91;Options#R4DVAR\|R4DVAR&#93;&#93; algorithms. This represents the diagonal elements of the full error analysis covariance matrix. These should be compared to the background error variances (squared standard deviations) for each variable which are specified in the &#91;&#91;Variables#STDnameI\|STDnameI&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;Variables#STDnameI\|STDnameI&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;Variables#STDnameB\|STDnameB&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;Variables#STDnameF\|STDnameF&#93;&#93; input NetCDF files. The analysis error covariance are written to a new NetCDF file (&#91;&#91;Variables#ERRname\|ERRname&#93;&#93;) which is specified in &#91;&#91;s4dvar.in&#93;&#93;. Currently, the analysis error is only possible for a single outer loop, &#91;&#91;Variables#Nouter\|Nouter&#93;&#93;=1.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;This option must never be run at the same time as &#91;&#91;Options#POSTERIOR_ERROR_I\|POSTERIOR_ERROR_I&#93;&#93;. If the &#91;&#91;Options#POSTERIOR_EOFS\|POSTERIOR_EOFS&#93;&#93; option is defined, the EOFS of the analysis error covariance matrix are also computed along with the trace estimate. | build-options-history:RBH-1296 | POSTERIOR_ERROR_I 설명 | `Options.wiki:2474` <code>:Option to compute the initial assimilation time-window posterior error covariance matrix for the &#91;&#91;Options#RBL4DVAR\|RBL4DVAR&#93;&#93; and &#91;&#91;Options#R4DVAR\|R4DVAR&#93;&#93; algorithms. This represents the diagonal elements of the full error analysis covariance matrix. These should be compared to the background error variances (squared standard deviations) for each variable which are specified in the &#91;&#91;Variables#STDnameI\|STDnameI&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;Variables#STDnameI\|STDnameI&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;Variables#STDnameB\|STDnameB&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;Variables#STDnameF\|STDnameF&#93;&#93; input NetCDF files. The analysis error covariance are written to a new NetCDF file (&#91;&#91;Variables#ERRname\|ERRname&#93;&#93;) which is specified in &#91;&#91;s4dvar.in&#93;&#93;. Currently, the analysis error is only possible for a single outer loop, &#91;&#91;Variables#Nouter\|Nouter&#93;&#93;=1.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;This option must never be run at the same time as &#91;&#91;Options#POSTERIOR_ERROR_F\|POSTERIOR_ERROR_F&#93;&#93;. If the &#91;&#91;Options#POSTERIOR_EOFS\|POSTERIOR_EOFS&#93;&#93; option is defined, the EOFS of the analysis error covariance matrix are also computed along with the trace estimate. SANITY_CHECK switch. INT(user(5)) => J-index of tangent variable to perturb           !`<br>`ROMS/Drivers/pert_roms.h:39` `! | build-options-history:RBH-1385 | SOUTH_FSNUDGING 설명 | `Options.wiki:2893` `:Option to use free-surface passive/active nudging term for the southern open boundary condition.` | `bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:357` `# if defined SOUTH_FSRADIATION`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:376` `#  ifdef SOUTH_FSNUDGING`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:377` `          IF ((dZdt*dZde).lt.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:378` `            tau=FSobc_in(ng,isouth)`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:379` `          ELSE`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:380` `            tau=FSobc_out(ng,isouth)`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:381` `          END IF`<br>`e8719fb7:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:369` `            IF (LBC(isouth,isFsur,ng)%nudging) THEN`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:457` `              IF (LBC(isouth,isFsur,ng)%nudging) THEN`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:439` `        IF (LBC(isouth,isFsur,ng)%radiation) THEN` | A 판 차이 | SOUTH_FSNUDGING의 설명은 roms-3.5의 CPP 경계 선택과 일치한다. 현재 대응 선택은 `LBC(isouth,isFsur,ng)%nudging`이다. 과거와 현재 모두 radiation 분기 안에서 nudging을 적용한다.<br>git 판정 근거: `e8719fb7` (2011-09-23). |

| build-options-history:RBH-1390 | SOUTH_FSRADIATION related | `Options.wiki:2906` <code>:'''related = ''' &#91;&#91;EAST_FSRADIATION&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;NORTH_FSRADIATION&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;WEST_FSRADIATION&#93;&#93;</code> | `bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:357` `# if defined SOUTH_FSRADIATION`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:231` `# if defined EAST_FSRADIATION`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:480` `# if defined NORTH_FSRADIATION`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:108` `# if defined WEST_FSRADIATION`<br>`e8719fb7:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:352` `        IF (LBC(isouth,isFsur,ng)%radiation) THEN`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:439` `        IF (LBC(isouth,isFsur,ng)%radiation) THEN` | A 판 차이 | SOUTH_FSRADIATION의 관련 옵션은 roms-3.5의 CPP 경계 선택과 일치한다. 현재 대응 선택은 `LBC(isouth,isFsur,ng)%radiation`이다.<br>git 판정 근거: `e8719fb7` (2011-09-23). |
| build-options-history:RBH-1391 | SOUTH_FSRADIATION conflict | `Options.wiki:2907` <code>:'''conflict = ''' &#91;&#91;NS_PERIODIC&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;SOUTH_FSCLAMPED&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;SOUTH_FSCHAPMAN&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;SOUTH_FSGRADIENT&#93;&#93;, &#91;&#91;SOUTHERN_WALL&#93;&#93;</code> | `bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:349` `#ifndef NS_PERIODIC`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:357` `# if defined SOUTH_FSRADIATION`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:418` `# elif defined SOUTH_FSCHAPMAN`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:436` `# elif defined SOUTH_FSCLAMPED`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:448` `# elif defined SOUTH_FSGRADIENT`<br>`bf4c05eb:ROMS/Nonlinear/bc_2d.F:232` `# ifdef SOUTHERN_WALL`<br>`e8719fb7:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:352` `        IF (LBC(isouth,isFsur,ng)%radiation) THEN`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/zetabc.F:439` `        IF (LBC(isouth,isFsur,ng)%radiation) THEN` | A 판 차이 | ROMS 3.5의 충돌 목록은 당시 CPP 경계 선택에 해당한다. 2011-09-23에 SOUTH_FSRADIATION 선택을 LBC 설정으로 바꿨다.<br>git 판정 근거: `bf4c05eb` (2011-09-22), `e8719fb7` (2011-09-23). |

TAlk = f(S[PSU])`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1189` `              Bio(i,k,iTAlk)=587.05_r8+50.56_r8*Bio(i,k,isalt)` | A 판 차이 | ROMS 3.1의 TALK_PROGNOSTIC은 알칼리도의 예측 계산을 선택했다. 2008-12-17에 선택 이름을 TALK_NONCONSERV로 바꿨다. 현재 BIO_FENNEL의 CARBON 경로에서 TALK_NONCONSERV를 끄면 염분에 따른 진단식으로 알칼리도를 계산한다.<br>git 판정 근거: `71bd6f1e` (2008-03-18), `8eda4413` (2008-12-17), `68580545` (2009-03-06), `19920857` (2009-09-11), `a4d1e9de` (2020-09-28). |

ANA_TCLIMA는 현재도 해석적 기후값 공급을 선택한다.<br>git 판정 근거: `0db3bdc5` (2013-04-18), `46f20897` (2014-03-13). |

| build-options-history:RBH-1536 | TKE_WAVEDISS 설명 | `Options.wiki:3346` <code>:Option to use wave breaking surface flux from wave amplitude for the Generic Length-Scale closure (&#91;&#91;Bibliography#WarnerJC_2005a\|Warner ''et al.'', 2005&#93;&#93;).</code> | `ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:3` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined GLS_MIXING && defined SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:890` `# if defined CRAIG_BANNER`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:896` `# elif defined TKE_WAVEDISS`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/set_avg.F:3` `#ifdef AVERAGES`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/set_avg.F:1247` `# if defined WAV_COUPLING \|\| defined TKE_WAVEDISS \|\| \`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/set_avg.F:1248` `    defined WDISS_THORGUZA \|\| defined WDISS_CHURTHOR`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:897` `          tke(i,j,N(ng),nnew)=MAX(cmu_fac4*(sz_alpha(ng)*               &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:898` `     &                            (Dissip_break(i,j)+                   &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:899` `     &                             Dissip_wcap (i,j)))**                &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:900` `     &                            (2.0_r8/3.0_r8), gls_Kmin(ng))`<br>`ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:259` `** TKE_WAVEDISS            if wave breaking surface flux from wave amplitude **`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/set_avg.F:1249` `        IF (Aout(idWdib,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:885` `!  Set Dirichlet surface and bottom boundary conditions. Compute`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:886` `!  surface roughness from wind stress (Charnok) and set Craig and`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:887` `!  Banner wave breaking surface flux, if appropriate.`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:888` `!`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:889` `        DO i=Istr,Iend`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:891` `          tke(i,j,N(ng),nnew)=MAX(cmu_fac4*0.5_r8*                      &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:892` `     &                            SQRT((sustr(i,j)+sustr(i+1,j))**2+    &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:893` `     &                                 (svstr(i,j)+svstr(i,j+1))**2)*   &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:894` `     &                            crgban_cw(ng)**(2.0_r8/3.0_r8),       &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:895` `     &                            gls_Kmin(ng))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:911` `# if defined CHARNOK`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:916` `# elif defined ZOS_HSIG`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:917` `          Zos_eff(i)=MAX(zos_hsig_alpha(ng)*Hwave(i,j), Zos_min)` | B 문서 오기 | NONLINEAR·GLS_MIXING·SOLVE3D에서 CRAIG_BANNER가 꺼지면 TKE_WAVEDISS는 Dissip_break와 Dissip_wcap으로 표면 TKE 경계값을 계산한다. 파고 Hwave를 쓰는 표면 거칠기는 CHARNOK이 꺼진 ZOS_HSIG 분기의 별도 계산이다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-1553 | TS_DIF2 설명 | `Options.wiki:3463` `:Option to` | `ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:65` `** TS_DIF2                 to turn ON or OFF harmonic horizontal mixing      **`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:47` `#  ifdef TS_DIF2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:48` `      USE t3dmix2_mod,        ONLY : t3dmix2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:3` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:46` `# ifndef TS_FIXED`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:106` `# ifndef TS_FIXED`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:107` `#  ifdef TS_DIF2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:113` `      CALL t3dmix2 (ng, tile)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:3` `#if !defined TS_FIXED && defined SOLVE3D && defined NONLINEAR && \`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:4` `    (defined TS_DIF2  \|\| defined TS_DIF4)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:18` `# ifdef TS_DIF2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:19` `#  if defined MIX_S_TS`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:20` `#   include "t3dmix2_s.h"`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:21` `#  elif defined MIX_GEO_TS`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:22` `#   include "t3dmix2_geo.h"`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:23` `#  elif defined MIX_ISO_TS`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:24` `#   include "t3dmix2_iso.h"`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:25` `#  else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:26` `      T3DMIX: must define one of MIX_S_TS, MIX_GEO_TS, MIX_ISO_TS`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix2_s.h:288` `              cff=dt(ng)*pm(i,j)*pn(i,j)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix2_s.h:289` `              cff1=cff*(FX(i+1,j  )-FX(i,j))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix2_s.h:290` `              cff2=cff*(FE(i  ,j+1)-FE(i,j))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix2_s.h:291` `              cff3=cff1+cff2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix2_s.h:292` `              t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc)=t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc)+cff3` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·SOLVE3D에서 TS_FIXED가 꺼지면 TS_DIF2는 추적자의 수평 조화 확산을 호출한다. 혼합 좌표는 MIX_S_TS·MIX_GEO_TS·MIX_ISO_TS 중 하나로 선택한다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-1555 | TS_FIXED 설명 | `Options.wiki:3481` `:Option to` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:3` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:46` `# ifndef TS_FIXED`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:47` `#  ifdef TS_DIF2`<br>`ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:68` `** TS_FIXED                if diagnostic run, no evolution of tracers        **`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:48` `      USE t3dmix2_mod,        ONLY : t3dmix2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1058` `# ifndef TS_FIXED`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1064` `            DO ig=1,GridsInLayer(nl)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1065` `              ng=GridNumber(ig,nl)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1066` `              DO tile=last_tile(ng),first_tile(ng),-1`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1067` `                CALL step3d_t (ng, tile)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1068` `              END DO`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1069` `!$OMP BARRIER`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1070` `            END DO`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:106` `# ifndef TS_FIXED`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:107` `#  ifdef TS_DIF2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:113` `      CALL t3dmix2 (ng, tile)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:115` `#  ifdef TS_DIF4`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:121` `      CALL t3dmix4 (ng, tile)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:123` `# endif` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·SOLVE3D에서 TS_FIXED를 켜면 rhs3d의 추적자 수평 확산 호출과 main3d의 step3d_t 호출을 제외한다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-1556 | TS_MPDATA 설명 | `Options.wiki:3490` `:Option to` | `51018275:ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:76` `** TS_MPDATA           use if recursive MPDATA 3D advection                  **`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:397` `        logical :: MPDATA         ! recursive flux corrected MPDATA`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1376` `        MPDATA : IF ((Hadvection(itrc,ng)%MPDATA).and.                  &`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1377` `     &               (Vadvection(itrc,ng)%MPDATA)) THEN` | C 문서 누락 | TS_MPDATA 절에는 기능 설명이 누락되어 있다. 현재 기능 노트에는 Hadvection과 Vadvection의 MPDATA 선택을 설명한다. 옵션의 변경 이력만으로 빈 설명을 A로 분류하지 않는다. |

Stop if activating obsolete tracers point Sources/Sinks option. This`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3835` `!  capability is now activated with standard input switch "LtracerSrc".`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3838` `        WRITE (stdout,250) uppercase('ts_psource'), 'LtracerSrc'`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3842` `        exit_flag=5`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:794` `                  IF (LtracerSrc(itrc,ng)) THEN` | C 문서 누락 | TS_PSOURCE 절은 이미 폐기 표시를 포함한다. 해당 행에는 기능 설명이 누락되어 있다. 현재 추적자 공급원 선택은 LtracerSrc로 설명한다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-1558 | TS_SMAGORINSKY 설명 | `Options.wiki:3508` `:Option to` | `ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:67` `** TS_SMAGORINSKY          to turn ON or OFF Smagorinsky-like diffusion      **`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:279` `# if defined UV_SMAGORINSKY \|\| defined UV_U3ADV_SPLIT \|\| \`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:280` `     defined TS_SMAGORINSKY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:5` `#if (defined DIFF_3DCOEF \|\| defined VISC_3DCOEF) && defined SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:388` `#  ifdef TS_SMAGORINSKY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:389` `!`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:390` `!  Smagorinsky diffusion at RHO-points.`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:391` `!`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:392` `#   ifdef TS_DIF2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:393` `            diff3d_r(i,j,k)=Hdiffusion(i,j)+                            &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:394` `     &                      SmagorCoef*omn(i,j)*DefRate`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:395` `#   elif defined TS_DIF4`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:396` `            diff3d_r(i,j,k)=Hdiffusion(i,j)+                            &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:397` `     &                      PecletCoef*(omn(i,j)**2)*DefRate`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:398` `#    ifdef MIX_GEO_TS`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:399` `            diff3d_r(i,j,k)=MIN(clip_scale, diff3d_r(i,j,k))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:400` `#    endif`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:401` `            diff3d_r(i,j,k)=SQRT(diff3d_r(i,j,k))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:402` `#   endif`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:403` `#   ifdef MASKING`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:404` `            diff3d_r(i,j,k)=diff3d_r(i,j,k)*rmask(i,j)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:405` `#   endif` | C 문서 누락 | SOLVE3D·DIFF_3DCOEF에서 TS_SMAGORINSKY는 추적자의 Smagorinsky 확산 계수를 계산한다. 계수식은 TS_DIF2와 TS_DIF4에 따라 갈린다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-1561 | TS_U3ADV_SPLIT 설명 | `Options.wiki:3526` `:Option to` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:109` `#  ifdef TS_U3ADV_SPLIT`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:110` `     &                   MIXING(ng) % diff3d_u,                         &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:111` `     &                   MIXING(ng) % diff3d_v,                         &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:5` `#if (defined DIFF_3DCOEF \|\| defined VISC_3DCOEF) && defined SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:107` `# ifdef DIFF_3DCOEF`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:437` `# if defined TS_U3ADV_SPLIT`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:440` `!  Compute tracer horizontal diffusion coefficient as the sum of the`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:441` `!  biharmonic diffusion term and a diapycnal hyperdiffusion term`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:442` `!  resulting from the splitting into advective and diffusive components`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:443` `!  the 3rd-order upstream bias horizontal advection.`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:466` `            diff3d_u(i,j,k)=0.5_r8*(Hdiffusion(i-1,j)+                  &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:467` `     &                              Hdiffusion(i  ,j))+                 &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:468` `     &                      PecletCoef*(om_u(i,j)**3)*                  &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:469` `     &                      ABS(u(i,j,k,nrhs))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:495` `            diff3d_v(i,j,k)=0.5_r8*(Hdiffusion(i,j-1)+                  &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:496` `     &                              Hdiffusion(i,j  ))+                 &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:497` `     &                      PecletCoef*(on_v(i,j)**3)*                  &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/hmixing.F:498` `     &                      ABS(v(i,j,k,nrhs))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix4_s.h:245` `#ifdef DIFF_3DCOEF`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix4_s.h:246` `# ifdef TS_U3ADV_SPLIT`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix4_s.h:247` `              cff=0.5_r8*diff3d_u(i,j,k)*pmon_u(i,j)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix4_s.h:248` `# else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:3` `#if !defined TS_FIXED && defined SOLVE3D && defined NONLINEAR && \`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:4` `    (defined TS_DIF2  \|\| defined TS_DIF4)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:30` `# ifdef TS_DIF4`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:31` `#  if defined MIX_S_TS`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/t3dmix.F:32` `#   include "t3dmix4_s.h"` | C 문서 누락 | SOLVE3D·DIFF_3DCOEF에서 TS_U3ADV_SPLIT는 hmixing의 속도 의존 확산 계수를 U·V 위치에 만든다. TS_DIF4 혼합 경로는 이 계수를 소비한다. 추적자 이류 연산자의 입력 선택과 이 CPP 확산 계수 분기를 구분한다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-1565 | UV_ADV 설명 | `Options.wiki:3555` `:Option to activate/deactivate horizontal and vertical advection of momentum.  If no additional options are specified, the default is to discretize the horizontal momentum with a third-order upstream bias advection scheme with velocity dependent hyper-viscosity and the vertical momentum with a fourth-order, centered differences scheme. Since there is some implicit viscosity associated with the default discretization, the user may avoid adding an explicity vicosity to the solution to suppress excisive smoothing.` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:3` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:743` `# ifdef UV_ADV`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:752` `#  ifdef UV_C2ADVECTION`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:788` `#  else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:812` `#   ifdef UV_C4ADVECTION`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:149` `# if defined CURVGRID && defined UV_ADV`<br>`ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:15` `**   The default horizontal advection is 3rd-order upstream bias for         **`<br>`ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:16` `**   3D momentum and 4th-order centered for 2D momentum. The default         **`<br>`ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:17` `**   vertical advection is 4th-order centered for 3D momentum. If this       **`<br>`ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:18` `**   is the case, no flags for momentum advection need to be activated.      **`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:816` `        cff=1.0_r8/6.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:820` `     &                        u(i+1,j,k,nrhs)-                          &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:825` `     &                        cff*(Huxx(i  ,j)+                         &`<br>`ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:33` `** UV_ADV                  to turn ON or OFF advection terms                 **`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:150` `     &                 GRID(ng) % dmde,                                 &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:151` `     &                 GRID(ng) % dndx,                                 &` | B 문서 오기 | UV_ADV 설명에서 3차원과 2차원 기본 이류를 구분한다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-1567 | UV_BAROCLINIC 설명 | `Options.wiki:3559` `:Option to` | `5c711cef:ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:520` `** UV_BAROCLINIC       use if assimilating baroclinic currents only          **`<br>`5c711cef:ROMS/Utility/oi_update.F:636` `#  ifndef UV_BAROCLINIC`<br>`5c711cef:ROMS/Utility/oi_update.F:638` `!  Set barotropic momentum to the vertically integrated values.` | C 문서 누락 | UV_BAROCLINIC 절에는 기능 설명이 누락되어 있다. 이 옵션은 과거 OI 동화에서 경압 성분만 갱신하는 선택이었다. 3차원 운동량 계산 자체를 켜는 옵션으로 설명하지 않는다. |

Qsrc       Mass transport profile (m3/s) of point Sources/Sinks.    !`<br>`fb1d4b49:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:963` `      IF (LuvSrc(ng)) THEN`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3845` `#ifdef UV_PSOURCE`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3851` `        WRITE (stdout,260) uppercase('uv_psource'), 'LuvSrc'`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3852` ` 260    FORMAT (/,' CHECKDEFS - cannot use obsolete option: ',a,        &`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3853` `     &          /,13x,'Use instead standard input switch: ',a,          &`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3854` `     &          /,13x,'Edit header file or build script!')`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3855` `        exit_flag=5`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1281` `      IF (LuvSrc(ng)) THEN`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1287` `            IF (INT(SOURCES(ng)%Dsrc(is)).eq.0) THEN`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1292` `                u(i,j,k,nnew)=SOURCES(ng)%Qsrc(is,k)*cff1`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1299` `                v(i,j,k,nnew)=SOURCES(ng)%Qsrc(is,k)*cff1` | A 판 차이 | ROMS 3.6은 UV_PSOURCE로 질량 유량 공급원 처리를 선택했다. 2013-11-25에 이 CPP 선택을 LuvSrc 입력으로 바꿨다. 현재 UV_PSOURCE를 정의하면 checkdefs가 오류를 설정한다.<br>git 판정 근거: `0db3bdc5` (2013-04-18), `fb1d4b49` (2013-11-25). |
